Dimensions0..256/result16384cells (constructor stays nonempty), copied cell attributes/cursor, selected immutable native grid events160..170, copied current-value snapshot before user dispatch, native proposed/old GetString retained. Bounded fixed-shape GRID WINDOW backed by native-owned default string table, dimensions1..256/total16384, cell text and row/column labels, whole-grid editable policy and enabled-editor readback. Native-backed CLOS/defaults/rollback, copied NUL-free programmatic text<=65536UTF8 bytes, zero-based indices. All scalar writers reject Lisp callbacks and active editors; committed reads remain available. Composite Input guards, early Destroy suppression and no managed handler-stack edits. No custom table/editor/renderer, grid events/selection/cursor/shape mutations or full wxGrid parity. Requires wxUSE_GRID/core; docs/GRID.md.

Bounded REPORT/SINGLE_SEL stored rows or native LC_VIRTUAL text hook. At most64 columns,100000 stored/10000000 virtual rows. Safe copied synchronous OnGetItemText response and checked owner/ancestor mutation guards. No native image/attribute callbacks, arbitrary item data, sorting, editing or complete class parity.

Virtual lists support only registered frame/panel/scroll/drawing/web/notebook/AUI/splitter ancestor chains. ABI72 adds visual notebook-group-pages and checked move-notebook-page-to-group.
